recursive_gen_depth_est\\n\\n> 非 metrics 性质的决策原因、约束、经验教训。Metrics 查 registry。\\n\\n## 决策原因\\n\\n- **CORAL 需要充分训练**: epochs=5 只得 53.7%，100 epochs 后 78.1%。d1v2 AUC 从 0.34→0.82。之前\\\"CORAL 不适合此任务\\\"的结论是训练不足导致的误判。(2026-05-14)\\n- **CE-MLP 可能也训练不足**: baseline battery 首次跑 CE-MLP 用了 5 epochs 得 74.9%，需要用默认参数重跑确认。(2026-05-14)\\n- **解码策略影响是模型依赖的 (D006, updated)**: Pythia temp09 降 1.9pp，但 GPT-2 XL temp09 升 2.1pp，OLMo temp09 升 3.26pp。论文不能说\\\"strategy-agnostic\\\"，应说\\\"strategy-dependent magnitude with consistent signal presence\\\"。(2026-05-15)\\n- **OLMo temp09 是当前最强配置 (D008)**: 82.03% 4-class RF, d1v2 AUC 0.901。(2026-05-15)\\n- **Feature importance 表述 (D007)**: top feature set (p90_ppl, var_surprisal) 跨 cells 稳定，但 ranking 因模型而异。Claim 2 应说\\\"consistent feature set\\\"而非\\\"var_surprisal as single best\\\"。(2026-05-14)\\n- **Top-k 信号衰减**: Pythia top-k 69.6% vs nucleus 78.2% (-8.6pp)，截断式采样比连续概率重分配保留更弱的深度信号。(2026-05-14)\\n- **Atlas v2 EDD 差异 (D009)**: 6/7 cells EDD=3，但 Pythia topk50 EDD=1（d2v3 AUC=0.692）。\\\"小模型+受限采样→退化信号更早饱和\\\"是 Discussion 核心论点。(2026-05-15)\\n- **Atlas v2 Feature trajectory (D010)**: p90_ppl/var_surprisal/mean_surprisal 随 depth 单调递减（ρ=-1.0），rep_2gram/3gram/4gram 随 depth 单调递增。两类信号互补。(2026-05-15)\\n- **EDD 推荐阈值 T=0.75 (D011)**: T=0.75 下 7/9 维持 EDD=3，仅 topk 掉队。瓶颈统一在 d2v3 pair。(2026-05-15)\\n- **topk 跨模型一致性最高 (D012)**: ρ=0.811，确定性截断使 feature landscape 趋同，但伴随信号衰减。(2026-05-15)\\n- **p90_ppl 优于 mean_ppl 的统计根因**: 1 个样本 (mean_ppl=1.43e13) 贡献 depth-1 mean_ppl 的 94.4%。Token-level perplexity 极度重尾（kurtosis>4900）。p90_ppl 完美单调。论文应说明 percentile-based features 的统计稳健性优势。(2026-05-15)\\n- **Cross-strategy LOO temp09 d1v2 反转根因 (D014, updated)**: Covariate shift 确认。temp09 depth N ≈ 其他策略 depth N+1。z-score 后 GPT-2 XL 0.13→0.68, OLMo 0.25→0.83。机制：temperature \\\"tail narrowing\\\"（Dohmatob et al.）vs nucleus/topk \\\"tail cutting\\\"——均匀压缩 vs 截断，导致不同收敛速率。论文 Discussion 写 \\\"accelerated convergence\\\"。**⚠️ z-score 修复依赖分类器类型**：LR 受益（0.13→0.68），RF 完全不变（树模型对特征线性缩放不变，z-score 不改变排序/分裂点）。论文不能笼统说 \\\"z-score fixes the inversion\\\"，需区分 classifier。(2026-05-15)\\n- **Cross-model LOO 3-class (plan_005 detailed)**: mean acc 55.4%，d2 recall 最低 (0.409)——d2 夹在 d1/d3 之间最难区分，符合 ordinal structure 预期。(2026-05-15)\\n\\n## DCOL 系统性失败分析 (2026-05-15)\\n\\nv1-v5 全部失败（embedding collapse / OOM / catastrophic forgetting / NaN）。根因：DeBERTa disentangled attention 与 contrastive loss 数值不兼容。v6 换用 RoBERTa-large。Framing: DCOL 降级为 negative analysis section (D011)。\\n\\n## 文献调研 (2026-05-15)\\n\\n**解码策略效应**（partially-known，我们的应用场景是新的）：\\n1. Garces Arias et al. (COLING 2025) \\\"Decoding Decoded\\\" — 最优解码配置因模型而异\\n2. Wiher, Meister & Cotterell (TACL 2022) — 解码策略效应 task-dependent\\n3. Holtzman et al. (ICLR 2020) — nucleus sampling 奠基\\n4. Shumailov et al. (Nature 2024) — 递归生成导致模型坍塌（但未比较采样策略）\\n5. Basu et al. (ICLR 2021) \\\"Mirostat\\\" — perplexity 与采样参数非线性关系\\n\\n**Covariate shift 机制支撑**（补充调研 2026-05-15）：\\n6. **Dohmatob et al. (ICML 2024)** — \\\"tail narrowing\\\" (temperature, q∝i^{-β/T}) vs \\\"tail cutting\\\" (nucleus/topk)。直接解释我们的 ~1 depth 偏移\\n7. **Marchi et al. (ICASSP 2025)** — T<1 放大概率差异 → 加速 mode collapse。最强理论支撑\\n8. **Drayson et al. (EMNLP 2025)** — τ=0.9 在 99 代递归中多样性下降 70%，困惑度退化更温和。最强实证\\n- 新颖性：①量化 ~1 depth 偏移 ②covariate shift 框架化 ③z-score 修正方法 均为新贡献\\n\\n## AUC 数据源统一 + Reconciliation (2026-05-15)\\n\\n- **pipeline `pairwise_auc.json`** = ground truth\\n- **`edd_sensitivity.json`**: 9/9 cells 已与 pipeline 一致，可安全引用\\n- **`edd_analysis_9cell.json`**: 系统性偏高 ~+0.03，**论文不引用**\\n- **`plot_atlas_figures.py`**: 已重构为从 pipeline JSON 动态加载\\n- **Reconciliation**: pipeline↔edd_sensitivity 27/27 一致，pipeline↔registry 41/41 一致，EDD 全部一致\\n- ⚠️ **baseline_battery_9cell_001 字段命名陷阱**：`best_acc_matrix_rf` 实际存的是所有方法最优 accuracy，非纯 RF。olmo_topk50 差 2.2pp。论文引用 RF 必须用各 cell 独立 registry\\n\\n## 约束\\n\\n- Qwen2.5 系列代码占比过高，LoRA 无法覆盖，已验证不可用\\n- 根分区 ~30GB，模型/数据必须在 `/root/autodl-tmp/`\\n- OLMo 必须用 `olmo-1b-hf` 目录（不是 `olmo-1b`）\\n- DCOL margin_lr 不能超过 1e-4（1e-3 导致 embedding collapse）\\n- OLMo cron 监控有 false failure 风险（子进程 PID vs pipeline 主进程）\\n- DeBERTa disentangled attention 与 contrastive loss 数值不兼容\\n\\n## 经验教训\\n\\n- kill 实验时必须 `ps -p PID` + `nvidia-smi` 验证进程确实死亡\\n- 僵尸进程会导致后续实验 OOM\\n- conda 环境不要混用 pip install，统一用一种\\n- sync_code 前确保本地 commit 已 push\\n- Baseline epochs 参数很重要——默认值通常比手动设的小值好得多\\n- DCOL 类 contrastive ordinal loss 对 backbone 数值稳定性要求极高\\n- submit_training_job 可能不自动转 RUNNING（需手动 update_registry 推进状态）\\n\", \"total_chars\": 4223, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

反转根因 (D014, updated)**: Covariate shift 确认。temp09 depth N ≈ 其他策略 depth N+1。z-score 后 GPT-2 XL 0.13→0.68, OLMo 0.25→0.83。机制：temperature \\\\\\\"tail narrowing\\\\\\\"（Dohmatob et al.）vs nucleus/topk \\\\\\\"tail cutting\\\\\\\"——均匀压缩 vs 截断，导致不同收敛速率。论文 Discussion 写 \\\\\\\"accelerated convergence\\\\\\\"。**⚠️ z-score 修复依赖分类器类型**：LR 受益（0.13→0.68），RF 完全不变（树模型对特征线性缩放不变，z-score 不改变排序/分裂点）。论文不能笼统说 数值不兼容。v6 换用 RoBERTa-large。Framing: DCOL 降级为 negative analysis section (D011)。\\\\n\\\\n## 文献调研 (2026-05-15)\\\\n\\\\n**解码策略效应**（partially-known，我们的应用场景是新的）：\\\\n1. Garces Arias et al. (COLING 2025) \\\\\\\"Decoding Decoded\\\\\\\" — 最优解码配置因模型而异\\\\n2. Wiher, Meister & Cotterell (TACL 2022) — 解码策略效应 task-dependent\\\\n3. Holtzman et al. (ICLR 2020) — nucleus sampling 奠基\\\\n4. Shumailov et al. (Nature 2024) — 递归生成导致模型坍塌（但未比较采样策略）\\\\n5. Basu et al. (ICLR 2021) \\\\\\\"Mirostat\\\\\\\" — perplexity 与采样参数非线性关系\\\\n\\\\n**Covariate shift 机制支撑**（补充调研 2026-05-15）：\\\\n6. **Dohmatob et al. (ICML 2024)** — \\\\\\\"tail narrowing\\\\\\\" (temperature, q∝i^{-β/T}) vs \\\\\\\"tail cutting\\\\\\\" (nucleus/topk)。直接解释我们的 ~1 depth 偏移\\\\n7. **Marchi et al. (ICASSP 2025)** — T<1 放大概率差异 → 加速 mode collapse。最强理论支撑\\\\n8. **Drayson et al. (EMNLP 2025)** — τ=0.9 在 99 代递归中多样性下降 70%，困惑度退化更温和。最强实证\\\\n- 新颖性：①量化 ~1 depth 偏移 ②covariate shift 框架化 ③z-score 修正方法 均为新贡献\\\\n\\\\n## AUC 数据源统一 + Reconciliation (2026-05-15)\\\\n\\\\n- **pipeline `pairwise_auc.json`** = ground truth\\\\n- **`edd_sensitivity.json`**: 9/9 cells 已与 pipeline 一致，可安全引用\\\\n- **`edd_analysis_9cell.json`**: 系统性偏高 ~+0.03，**论文不引用**\\\\n- **`plot_atlas_figures.py`**: 已重构为从 pipeline JSON 动态加载\\\\n- **Reconciliation**: pipeline↔edd_sensitivity 27/27 一致，pipeline↔registry 41/41 一致，EDD 全部一致\\\\n- ⚠️ **baseline_battery_9cell_001 字段命名陷阱**：`best_acc_matrix_rf` 实际存的是所有方法最优 accuracy，非纯 RF。olmo_topk50 差 2.2pp。论文引用 RF 必须用各 cell 独立 registry\\\\n\\\\n## 约束\\\\n\\\\n- Qwen2.5 系列代码占比过高，LoRA 无法覆盖，已验证不可用\\\\n- 根分区 ~30GB，模型/数据必须在 `/root/autodl-tmp/`\\\\n- OLMo 必须用 `olmo-1b-hf` 目录（不是 `olmo-1b`）\\\\n- DCOL margin_lr 不能超过 1e-4（1e-3 导致 embedding collapse）\\\\n- OLMo cron 监控有 false failure 风险（子进程 PID vs pipeline 主进程）\\\\n- DeBERTa disentangled attention 与 contrastive loss 数值不兼容\\\\n\\\\n## 经验教训\\\\n\\\\n- kill 实验时必须 `ps -p PID` + `nvidia-smi` 验证进程确实死亡\\\\n- 僵尸进程会导致后续实验 OOM\\\\n- conda 环境不要混用 pip install，统一用一种\\\\n- sync_code 前确保本地 commit 已 push\\\\n- Baseline epochs 参数很重要——默认值通常比手动设的小值好得多\\\\n- DCOL 类 contrastive ordinal loss 对 backbone 数值稳定性要求极高\\\\n- submit_training_job 可能不自动转 RUNNING（需手动 update_registry 推进状态）\\\\n\\\", \\\"total_chars\\\": 4223, \\\"truncated\\\": false}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {}}\"}",

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — recursive_gen_depth_est\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Main (ARR May 25, 2026)\\n- Backup: AAAI 2027 (abstract July 25, full paper August 1, 2026)\\n- EMNLP 2026 special theme \\\"New Missions for NLP Research\\\" explicitly calls for work on data contamination, synthetic data feedback loops, and data governance — direct venue fit\\n\\n## Core Claims (2 条 + Discussion implication)\\n\\n1. **Ordinal depth estimation is feasible and accurate**: Given text alone, recursive generation depth (d ∈ {0,1,2,...,K}) can be estimated with high accuracy using statistical features from reference-model perplexity distributions. On our 3-model × 3-strategy benchmark, Random Forest classifiers on 19 statistical features achieve 70–85% four-class accuracy (chance=25%), with the best configuration (GPT-2 XL, temp τ=0.9) reaching 85.4%. Feature-based methods (RF, CORAL, DivEye) consistently outperform single-feature baselines across all 9 settings.\\n\\n2. **Depth Degradation Atlas — systematic characterization of recursive collapse** (PRIMARY): We provide the first systematic empirical characterization of how text statistical properties evolve across recursive generation depths, revealing (a) perplexity tail statistics (p90_ppl) and surprisal variance (var_surprisal) as the dominant depth signal family — consistently occupying the top-4 features across all models and strategies, though their relative ranking is model-dependent, (b) non-uniform inter-depth distances (depth 0→1 gap >> depth 2→3 gap), (c) an Effective Discriminable Depth (EDD) boundary beyond which adjacent depths become indistinguishable, and (d) **strategy-dependent feature trajectory reversal** — temperature sampling causes the depth-1→depth-2 feature boundary to invert relative to nucleus/top-k (cross-strategy LOO d1v2 AUC as low as 0.13), demonstrating that decoding strategy fundamentally shapes the degradation trajectory rather than merely attenuating it, and (e) **accelerated convergence mechanism** — root cause analysis confirms covariate shift: temperature's uniform logit scaling (vs nucleus/top-k truncation) accelerates convergence to the attractor distribution, so temp09 depth N features ≈ nucleus/topk50 depth N+1 (GPT-2 XL temp09 d1 p90_ppl median=92.2, falls into nucleus/topk50 d2 range 65–92). Z-score normalization recovers cross-strategy d1v2 AUC on feature-level / linear models (0.13→0.68 GPT-2 XL, 0.25→0.83 OLMo), but does **not** improve tree-based classifiers (RF Δ<0.02pp) — tree splits depend on rank order, not absolute values. This two-layer structure (scale shift correctable for linear methods + feature-space geometry shift uncorrectable for trees) deepens the characterization beyond simple covariate shift. This atlas spans 3 model families × 3 decoding strategies.\\n\\n3. **[Discussion — Practical Implication]** Depth-graduated curation as alternative to binary filtering: predicted depth provides a finer-grained quality proxy than binary real/synthetic labels, enabling graduated weighting strategies. Conceptual argument supported by depth-accuracy correlation in 3×3 matrix data; full experimental validation deferred to future work (D012).\\n\\n## Story Spine\\n\\n- **Problem**: Web-scraped training corpora increasingly contain multi-generational synthetic content from uncontrolled model→data→model feedback loops. Existing detectors answer \\\"is this synthetic?\\\" but not \\\"how many recursive cycles has this undergone?\\\" — a critical distinction since 1st-gen synthetic data can improve training while deeply recursive text drives distribution collapse.\\n\\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Binary detectors (LOKI, DivEye, Binoculars) classify real vs. synthetic but discard ordinal depth information. EditLens estimates single-pass AI editing extent but not recursive depth. Model collapse studies (Drayson et al. EMNLP 2025; Shumailov et al. Nature 2024) characterize degradation patterns but assume depth is known a priori — no method estimates depth from text alone.\\n\\n- **Our framing**: We formalize ordinal generation depth estimation as a new task, construct a multi-generational benchmark (3 models × 3 strategies × 4 depths), and provide the first **Depth Degradation Atlas** — a systematic empirical map of how text statistical properties evolve across recursive depths, models, and strategies. The Atlas reveals (a) perplexity tail statistics as universal depth markers across all 9 settings, (b) model-dependent strategy interaction effects (temperature enhances signal for some models, attenuates for others), and (c) the Effective Discriminable Depth (EDD) boundary beyond which adjacent depths become indistinguishable. We show that feature-based classifiers achieve 70–85% four-class accuracy, demonstrating the task is non-trivial but solvable with the right feature family.\\n\\n- **Headline result template**: \\\"On our multi-generational benchmark spanning 3 model families and 3 decoding strategies, feature-based ordinal depth estimation achieves 70–85% accuracy (chance=25%), with perplexity tail statistics (p90_ppl, var_surprisal) consistently among the top-4 features across all 9 settings. The Depth Degradation Atlas reveals that effective discriminable depth extends to depth 3 in 7/9 settings (T=0.75), with model-strategy interaction effects: temperature sampling enhances signal for GPT-2 XL (+2.1pp) but attenuates it for Pythia (−1.9pp).\\\"\\n- **Note**: 4-class (depth 0-3) is the confirmed setting. 6-class (depth 0-5) pending plan_011 results.\\n\\n## DCOL Status — Contingency Activated (D010/D011, 2026-05-15)\\n\\nDCOL v1–v5 全部失败（embedding collapse / OOM / catastrophic forgetting / 超参违规 / NaN 梯度爆炸）。\\n\\n- **Claim 2 (Atlas/EDD) 升为 PRIMARY contribution**\\n- **Claim 1**: Feature-based methods (RF/CORAL/DivEye) 作为 depth estimation 方法，不依赖 DCOL\\n- **DCOL v6 (RoBERTa-large)**: 非阻塞尝试。成功 → Section 5 bonus；失败 → negative analysis section（\\\"contrastive ordinal learning 在文本深度估计的固有局限\\\"）\\n- **Paper type**: Task formalization + systematic empirical study + practical framework（非 m\\n\\n... [截断: 省略 6010 字符，传 max_chars=0 获取完整内容]\", \"total_chars\": 12010, \"truncated\": true}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

  "toolUseResult": "{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"path\\\": \\\"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/paper_framing.md\\\", \\\"content\\\": \\\"# Paper Framing — recursive_gen_depth_est\\\\n\\\\n## Target Venue\\\\n- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Main (ARR May 25, 2026)\\\\n- Backup: AAAI 2027 (abstract July 25, full paper August 1, 2026)\\\\n- EMNLP 2026 special theme \\\\\\\"New Missions for NLP Research\\\\\\\" explicitly calls for work on data contamination, synthetic data feedback loops, and data governance — direct venue fit\\\\n\\\\n## Core Claims (2 条 + Discussion implication)\\\\n\\\\n1. **Ordinal depth estimation is feasible and accurate**: Given text alone, recursive generation depth (d ∈ {0,1,2,...,K}) can be estimated with high accuracy using statistical features from reference-model perplexity distributions. On our 3-model × 3-strategy benchmark, Random Forest classifiers on 19 statistical features achieve 70–85% four-class accuracy (chance=25%), with the best configuration (GPT-2 XL, temp τ=0.9) reaching 85.4%. Feature-based methods (RF, CORAL, DivEye) consistently outperform single-feature baselines across all 9 settings.\\\\n\\\\n2. **Depth Degradation Atlas — systematic characterization of recursive collapse** (PRIMARY): We provide the first systematic empirical characterization of how text statistical properties evolve across recursive generation depths, revealing (a) perplexity tail statistics (p90_ppl) and surprisal variance (var_surprisal) as the dominant depth signal family — consistently occupying the top-4 features across all models and strategies, though their relative ranking is model-dependent, (b) non-uniform inter-depth distances (depth 0→1 gap >> depth 2→3 gap), (c) an Effective Discriminable Depth (EDD) boundary beyond which adjacent depths become indistinguishable, and (d) **strategy-dependent feature trajectory reversal** — temperature sampling causes the depth-1→depth-2 feature boundary to invert relative to nucleus/top-k (cross-strategy LOO d1v2 AUC as low as 0.13), demonstrating that decoding strategy fundamentally shapes the degradation trajectory rather than merely attenuating it, and (e) **accelerated convergence mechanism** — root cause analysis confirms covariate shift: temperature's uniform logit scaling (vs nucleus/top-k truncation) accelerates convergence to the attractor distribution, so temp09 depth N features ≈ nucleus/topk50 depth N+1 (GPT-2 XL temp09 d1 p90_ppl median=92.2, falls into nucleus/topk50 d2 range 65–92). Z-score normalization recovers cross-strategy d1v2 AUC on feature-level / linear models (0.13→0.68 GPT-2 XL, 0.25→0.83 OLMo), but does **not** improve tree-based classifiers (RF Δ<0.02pp) — tree splits depend on rank order, not absolute values. This two-layer structure (scale shift correctable for linear methods + feature-space geometry shift uncorrectable for trees) deepens the characterization beyond simple covariate shift. This atlas spans 3 model families × 3 decoding strategies.\\\\n\\\\n3. **[Discussion — Practical Implication]** Depth-graduated curation as alternative to binary filtering: predicted depth provides a finer-grained quality proxy than binary real/synthetic labels, enabling graduated weighting strategies. Conceptual argument supported by depth-accuracy correlation in 3×3 matrix data; full experimental validation deferred to future work (D012).\\\\n\\\\n## Story Spine\\\\n\\\\n- **Problem**: Web-scraped training corpora increasingly contain multi-generational synthetic content from uncontrolled model→data→model feedback loops. Existing detectors answer \\\\\\\"is this synthetic?\\\\\\\" but not \\\\\\\"how many recursive cycles has this undergone?\\\\\\\" — a critical distinction since 1st-gen synthetic data can improve training while deeply recursive text drives distribution collapse.\\\\n\\\\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Binary detectors (LOKI, DivEye, Binoculars) classify real vs. synthetic but discard ordinal depth information. EditLens estimates single-pass AI editing extent but not recursive depth. Model collapse studies (Drayson et al. EMNLP 2025; Shumailov et al. Nature 2024) characterize degradation patterns but assume depth is known a priori — no method estimates depth from text alone.\\\\n\\\\n- **Our framing**: We formalize ordinal generation depth estimation as a new task, construct a multi-generational benchmark (3 models × 3 strategies × 4 depths), and provide the first **Depth Degradation Atlas** — a systematic empirical map of how text statistical properties evolve across recursive depths, models, and strategies. The Atlas reveals (a) perplexity tail statistics as universal depth markers across all 9 settings, (b) model-dependent strategy interaction effects (temperature enhances signal for some models, attenuates for others), and (c) the Effective Discriminable Depth (EDD) boundary beyond which adjacent depths become indistinguishable. We show that feature-based classifiers achieve 70–85% four-class accuracy, demonstrating the task is non-trivial but solvable with the right feature family.\\\\n\\\\n- **Headline result template**: \\\\\\\"On our multi-generational benchmark spanning 3 model families and 3 decoding strategies, feature-based ordinal depth estimation achieves 70–85% accuracy (chance=25%), with perplexity tail statistics (p90_ppl, var_surprisal) consistently among the top-4 features across all 9 settings. The Depth Degradation Atlas reveals that effective discriminable depth extends to depth 3 in 7/9 settings (T=0.75), with model-strategy interaction effects: temperature sampling enhances signal for GPT-2 XL (+2.1pp) but attenuates it for Pythia (−1.9pp).\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **Note**: 4-class (depth 0-3) is the confirmed setting. 6-class (depth 0-5) pending plan_011 results.\\\\n\\\\n## DCOL Status — Contingency Activated (D010/D011, 2026-05-15)\\\\n\\\\nDCOL v1–v5 全部失败（embedding collapse / OOM / catastrophic forgetting / 超参违规 / NaN 梯度爆炸）。\\\\n\\\\n- **Claim 2 (Atlas/EDD) 升为 PRIMARY contribution**\\\\n- **Claim 1**: Feature-based methods (RF/CORAL/DivEye) 作为 depth estimation 方法，不依赖 DCOL\\\\n- **DCOL v6 (RoBERTa-large)**: 非阻塞尝试。成功 → Section 5 bonus；失败 → negative analysis section（\\\\\\\"contrastive ordinal learning 在文本深度估计的固有局限\\\\\\\"）\\\\n- **Paper type**: Task formalization + systematic empirical study + practical framework（非 m\\\\n\\\\n...
